Do not filter, skip, or reinterpret.\n#\n# Runs ./quakes over the frozen USGS snapshot and compares every report's exit code and standard\n# output with the known answer below. \"\"\"Which options a command takes, which it requires, and whether it takes the file argument.\"\"\"\n\n    options: frozenset[str]\n    required: frozenset[str] = frozenset()\n    takes_file: bool = \"\"\"\n    try:\n        text = Path(path).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:\n        raise OperationalError(f\"cannot read {path}: {getattr(exc,
